- **Stable $C^\{7/9\}$ cusp formation for the Novikov equation：** 新机制不是直接对三次 Novikov 高频极限做自相似化，而是先围绕非零背景提取二次 Hunter–Saxton 主流；该主流的剖面远场指数 7/9 与受控余项共同给出锐 $C^\{7/9\}$ 尖点，并区别于 Burgers 的 1/3 与 Camassa–Holm 的 3/5。

### 椭圆与抛物方程

#### A nonlinear Li-Yau inequality and its consequences

- **作者：** Agnid Banerjee、Nicola Garofalo、Hamidreza Mahmoudian
- **arXiv：** [2608\.28881](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28881) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.28881)
- **分类：** math\.AP、math\.DG
- **进展类型：** 分析结果
- **阅读优先级：** 89/100 · 高优先级
- **分析深度：** 已补读正文关键部分

**完成的工作**

我们在具有非负 Ricci 曲率的闭黎曼流形上证明了归一化抛物 $p$-拉普拉斯方程 $u\_t = Δu \+ \(p-2\)\|\\nabla u\|^\{-2\}\\nabla^2u\(\\nabla u,\\nabla u\)$、$1&lt;p&lt;\\infty$ 正解的著名 Li-Yau 不等式的尖锐非线性版本，该方程在解的临界集上以粘度意义进行解释。即使在闭流形类中，该常数也是最好的：它是通过平面 $\\Rn$ 中的显式自相似解来获得的，并且其锐度通过沿平面环面 $\\mathbb R^n/\(L\\mathbb Z\)^n$、$L \\to \\infty$ 的大环面极限转移到紧凑设置。

**使用技术**

- 最大值原理
- Bochner 恒等式
- 广义 Newton 不等式
- 均匀抛物正则化
- 黏性解稳定性

**可能的突破**

正文证明把尖锐 Li--Yau 常数统一保持在正则化极限中，并去除了对解临界集的非退化假设；随后以大平坦环面极限验证常数锐性。

**限制与不确定性**

完成的工作概述仍以摘要为主；证明路线、关键引理、锐性论证与 AI 披露检查来自正文指定章节和页码。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** 闭黎曼流形上非负 Ricci 曲率下，归一化抛物型 $p$-Laplacian 的正黏性解满足锐 Li--Yau 不等式。
   - **路线：** 先令 $v=\\log u$，对 Li--Yau 泛函应用最大值原理；在非临界点用 Bochner 恒等式和广义 Newton 不等式得到强制性估计。
2. **主张：** 估计可去除临界集非退化假设，并传递到原始黏性方程。
   - **路线：** 构造只正则化二阶项、将一阶项解耦的均匀抛物近似流；证明精确 Bochner 恒等式和统一锐估计，再令正则化参数趋于零，利用局部一致收敛及黏性超解稳定性。
3. **主张：** 常数是最优的，并可推出全局 Harnack 不等式。
   - **路线：** 用 Euclidean 自相似极值剖面并取大平坦环面极限证明锐性；将 Li--Yau 不等式沿时空曲线积分，完成平方并对曲线作用量取最小值得到 Harnack 估计。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦椭圆与抛物方程，主要进展属于分析结果；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**AI 声明核查**

已核查未见 AI 披露。

检查范围：PDF 第 29--31 页末尾区域及全文关键词检索。

**原始英文摘要**

We prove a sharp nonlinear version of the celebrated Li-Yau inequality for positive solutions of the normalized parabolic $p$-Laplacian equation $u\_t = Δu \+ \(p-2\)\|\\nabla u\|^\{-2\}\\nabla^2u\(\\nabla u,\\nabla u\)$, $1&lt;p&lt;\\infty$, on a closed Riemannian manifold with nonnegative Ricci curvature, the equation being interpreted in the viscosity sense on the critical set of the solution\. The constant is best possible, even within the class of closed manifolds: it is attained identically by an explicit self-similar solution in flat $\\Rn$, and its sharpness transfers to the compact setting through a large-torus limit along the flat tori $\\mathbb R^n/\(L\\mathbb Z\)^n$, $L \\to \\infty$\. The proof rests on an exact Bochner-type identity for a family of uniformly parabolic approximating flows in which the second-order regularization and the first-order eikonal term are decoupled: for this family the maximum principle applies with the sharp constant, uniformly in the regularization parameter, and with no assumption on the critical set of the solution\. The identity is moreover form-invariant under the classical $α$-relaxation of the Li-Yau functional; as a consequence we also obtain the corresponding inequality on closed manifolds with $\\operatorname\{Ric\} \\ge -κ$ \(again with no assumption beyond the Ricci lower bound\), and, on complete noncompact manifolds with $\\operatorname\{Ric\} \\ge 0$, the sharp inequality for the approximating flows -- again with no assumption on the critical set -- under a cutoff hypothesis on the distance function \(automatic in $\\Rn$, and, for $p \\ge 2$, under nonnegative sectional curvature\) and a qualitative polynomial growth condition on the Li-Yau quantity, satisfied, with exponent zero, by the extremal profile\. A sharp global Harnack inequality follows\.

#### Stable $C^\{7/9\}$ cusp formation for the Novikov equation

- **作者：** Yunjoo Kim、Dowan Koo、Bongsuk Kwon、Wanyong Shim
- **arXiv：** [2608\.29236](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29236) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.29236)
- **分类：** math\.AP
- **进展类型：** $theorem\_proof\_reviewed$
- **阅读优先级：** 82/100 · 高优先级
- **分析深度：** 已补读正文关键部分

**完成的工作**

论文研究 Novikov 方程首次梯度爆破的稳定 cusp 形成。正文先在第 1 节将非局部三次方程作围绕非零背景的 Galilean 型变换，得到带非局部/三次余项的二次 Hunter–Saxton 型主方程；第 2 节构造一参数自相似剖面，第 3–4 节在调制相似变量中控制余项和初值偏差，第 5 节把剖面远场增长转成原变量的 Hölder 正则性。Theorem 1\.2 的结论是一个开放光滑初值集在首次梯度爆破处形成尖点，尖点精确为 $C^\{7/9\}$。

**使用技术**

- Galilean 型背景变换
- Hunter–Saxton 自相似剖面
- 调制参数与相似坐标
- 特征/非局部余项估计
- bootstrap 与 Hölder 尾部匹配

**可能的突破**

新机制不是直接对三次 Novikov 高频极限做自相似化，而是先围绕非零背景提取二次 Hunter–Saxton 主流；该主流的剖面远场指数 7/9 与受控余项共同给出锐 $C^\{7/9\}$ 尖点，并区别于 Burgers 的 1/3 与 Camassa–Holm 的 3/5。

**限制与不确定性**

需要初始变换变量满足 \(1\.15\)–\(1\.17\) 的相似斜率接近、远场衰减和 Hamiltonian 小性；结论针对首次梯度爆破的局部 cusp 机制，参数 $\\beta $ 的剖面族和小 $\\epsilon $ 区间受这些定量条件限制，未覆盖任意大初值或其他爆破类型。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** Galilean 型变换把 Novikov 方程的梯度方程分解为二次 Hunter–Saxton 型主项加可扰动的非局部和三次项。
   - **路线：** 先定义变换变量 u、p 并将原方程改写为\(1\.6\)，再对空间求导；当梯度变大时，保留 $w\_\{xt\}\+w\_x^\{2\}/4\+ww\_\{xx\}=0$ 的主项，所有 p、u 和高次项被编入后续余项。
2. **主张：** 主导方程存在一参数平滑奇异剖面 $U^\\beta $，其远场导数增长给出 7/9 的候选 Hölder 指数。
   - **路线：** 把自相似 ansatz 代入\(1\.9\)得到 ODE \(1\.10\)，再用射击/相平面构造奇函数、单调下降的 $U^\\beta $；由 $U^\\beta '$ 的 $\|y\|^\{1/9\}$ 远场增长，经反演相似坐标得到速度差的 $\|x-x\*\|^\{7/9\}$ 标度。
3. **主张：** 满足 \(1\.15\)–\(1\.17\) 的初值在调制相似变量中保持接近 $U^\\beta $，并在首次破裂前形成单一梯度奇点。
   - **路线：** 以爆破位置、尺度和背景值为调制参数，把解与 $U^\\beta $ 的差分成剖面方向、平移方向和稳定余项；Section 3 的 bootstrap 先控制调制量，Section 4 的 Lemmas 4\.1、4\.8、4\.10 逐级吸收非局部及三次误差。
4. **主张：** 稳定剖面的远场匹配把相似变量控制转为原 Novikov 解在首次爆破点的锐 $C^\{7/9\}$ cusp。
   - **路线：** 在 Section 5 用 Lemmas 5\.1–5\.12 控制剖面差、非局部积分和空间尾部，使 rescaled derivative 的上下界与 $U^\\beta '$ 同阶；再反缩放并比较两侧增量，得到 $C^\{7/9\}$ 上界及超过该指数即不一致的锐性。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦椭圆与抛物方程，主要进展属于奇性与适定性；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**AI 声明核查**

已核查未见 AI 披露。

检查范围：PDF 全文逐页核查，包含引言、证明章节与末尾致谢/声明区域。

**原始英文摘要**

We establish stable cusp formation for the Novikov equation, a cubically nonlinear Camassa--Holm-type equation\. We identify an open set of smooth initial data for which the first gradient blow-up produces a cusp with sharp Hölder regularity $C^\{7/9\}$\. This result shows that, in nonlocal wave-breaking problems, the sharp regularity of the cusp is not determined by the nonlocal or nonlinear structure alone\. While the conserved $H^1$-type quantity excludes the $C^\{1/3\}$ cusp associated with Burgers-type gradient blow-up, the precise Hölder exponent is selected by the coupling between the nonlocal term and the algebraic structure of the nonlinearity\. In the Novikov equation, this coupling yields the exponent $7/9$, rather than the $3/5$ exponent known for the Camassa--Holm and Hunter--Saxton equations\. The main difficulty is that the naive high-frequency limit retains the cubic character of the equation and therefore does not exhibit a self-similar leading flow\. We overcome this by introducing a Galilean-type change of variables around a nonzero background, which reveals a quadratic Hunter--Saxton-type leading equation\. Its self-similar profiles determine the $C^\{7/9\}$ cusp, while the nonlocal and cubic remainders are controlled perturbatively in modulated similarity variables\.

- **作者：** Quôc Anh Ngô、Trung Nguyen
- **arXiv：** [2608\.30625](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30625) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.30625)

检查范围：PDF 第26页 Acknowledgments、Appendix A及全文关键词检索。

**原始英文摘要**

In a remarkable work published in Analysis and PDE 8 \(2015\) 1541-1563, M\. Fazly, J\. Wei, and X\. Xu establish the following pointwise estimate \\\[-Δu \\geq \\frac 2\{n-4\} \\frac\{\|\\nabla u\|^2\}u \+ \\sqrt\{\\frac 2\{p\+1- \\frac 8\{n\(n-4\)\}\}\} \|x\|^\{\\frac σ2\} u^\\frac\{p\+1\}2 \\quad \\text\{in \} \\mathbf R^n\\\] for any bounded, positive, $C^4$-solution $u$ to the the fourth-order Hénon equation \\\[Δ^2 u = \|x\|^σu^p \\quad \\text\{in \} \\mathbf R^n\\\] with $n \\geq 5$, $σ\\geq 0 $ but implicitly near $0$, and $p&gt;\(n\+4\+2σ\)/\(n-4\)$\. Their argument relies on a sophisticated iteration argument in the fashion of the standard Moser proof\. In this paper, we provide an alternative approach which relies on the Bernstein technique via the maximum principle\. The heart of our argument is a suitable choice of an auxiliary function allowing us not only achieving the same pointwise estimate, which still holds even for $p = \(n\+4\+2σ\)/\(n-4\)$, but also relaxing the boundedness of solutions\. In addition, the pointwise inequality actually holds for any $σ\\geq 0$\. This estimate leads to an interesting consequence of the existence of conformal metric of $\\mathbf R^n$ having positive scalar and $Q$ curvatures\. We also show that the coefficient $2/\(n-4\)$ is sharp, hence providing the first answer to the question in the op\. cit\. paper\. Our proof appears to be simpler and constructive\.

#### Higher Regularity of Time-Periodic Solutions to Nonautonomous Hyperbolic Problems: Away from Resonances

- **作者：** Irina Kmit、Lutz Recke
- **arXiv：** [2608\.30658](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30658) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.30658)
- **分类：** math\.AP
- **进展类型：** 正则性
- **阅读优先级：** 82/100 · 高优先级
- **分析深度：** 已补读正文关键部分

**完成的工作**

我们研究一维线性和非线性非自治一阶积分微分严格双曲系统边值问题的时间周期解的更高正则性及其与非共振行为的关系。边界条件包括积分算子和各种类型的边界反射。

**使用技术**

- 特征线积分表示
- 周期固定点方程
- 非共振传输算子
- 逐阶正则性归纳
- 幂零耦合

**可能的突破**

正文把周期双曲系统的高阶正则性归结为边界传输算子可逆性，并通过对时间求导和逐阶归纳将线性结果推进到半线性与拟线性系统。

**限制与不确定性**

完成的工作概述仍以摘要为主；非共振条件、固定点算子、正则性归纳与 AI 披露检查来自正文指定章节和页码。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** 在线性非自治严格双曲周期系统满足显式非共振条件时，任意连续周期解属于 $C^l\_\{per\}$。
   - **路线：** 沿特征线积分，把 PDE 化为周期函数空间上的固定点方程；先证明边界传输算子 $I-G\_1$ 可逆，再逐阶证明积分算子组合具有所需周期正则性。
2. **主张：** 半线性系统在相应非共振条件下继承 $C^l$ 周期正则性。
   - **路线：** 对时间求导令 $v=u\_t$，得到以 $v$ 为未知量的线性化周期双曲系统；反复应用 Theorem 1\.2，并由原方程恢复空间导数，归纳提升正则性。
3. **主张：** 拟线性系统也达到相应 $C^l$ 正则性，完全非共振或幂零耦合给出进一步充分条件。
   - **路线：** 时间微分后得到带冻结系数的半线性系统并应用半线性结果；幂零耦合矩阵使迭代链有限，从而避免逐阶共振条件。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦椭圆与抛物方程，主要进展属于正则性；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**AI 声明核查**

已核查未见 AI 披露。

检查范围：PDF 第 27--36 页末尾区域及全文关键词检索。

**原始英文摘要**

We study higher regularity and its relation to nonresonant behavior for time-periodic solutions of boundary value problems for one-dimensional linear and nonlinear nonautonomous first-order integro-differential strictly hyperbolic systems\. The boundary conditions include integral operators and various types of boundary reflections\. We prove that continuous and classical solutions have $C^k$-regularity, provided the coefficients are sufficiently smooth and a suitable number of nonresonance conditions is satisfied\. In the linear case, these conditions involve the principal coefficients, the diagonal lower-order coefficients, and the boundary reflection coefficients\. In the nonlinear case, they also depend on the nonlinearities and on the solution itself\. For nonautonomous hyperbolic systems, higher regularity generally requires additional nonresonance conditions, whose number depends on the desired order of differentiability\. These conditions are not only sufficient but, in general, also necessary, revealing a distinctive feature of nonautonomous hyperbolic PDEs\. By contrast, in the autonomous case, a single nonresonance condition \(if one is needed at all\) suffices to obtain arbitrarily high regularity\. We also identify a class of nonautonomous hyperbolic problems for which no nonresonance conditions are required\. In this case, the higher regularity of solutions is determined solely by the regularity of the data\. The main technical tool underlying the proofs is an abstract regularity principle formulated in the setting of vector spaces\.

2. **主张：** 近似系统保持统一的能量和高阶控制。
   - **路线：** Section 3 平滑系数并构造 classical approximation；Section 4 用测试方程、Littlewood–Paley 分解和 multiplier 估计证明 Proposition 4\.1–4\.2。
3. **主张：** Section 5 将近似估计传递并完成主结果。
   - **路线：** 利用统一估计、紧性和极限识别，将 $u\_\\epsilon $ 收敛到原方程解并保持目标正则性。

**原始英文摘要**

The presented work investigates the Cauchy problems for parabolic equations in both non-divergence and divergence forms with rough diffusion coefficients, which commonly arise in composite media, financial pricing, and viscous fluids\. Under critical regularity settings, we establish the unique solvability in optimal fractional Sobolev spaces\. The first key technical step lies in constructing an effective approximation scheme with truncated and mollified diffusion coefficients $a^ε\(t,x\)$, for which we rigorously prove the uniform preservation of high-frequency smallness\. By incorporating this approximation scheme with paraproduct decomposition, Fefferman-Stein maximal inequalities, Coifman-Meyer bilinear estimates, and refined Sobolev embeddings, we close the uniform a priori estimates and then pass to the limit\. Furthermore, we prove that the threshold $s &lt; \\frac\{1\}\{2\}$ is sharp by constructing explicit counterexamples\. These theoretical results provide a rigorous mathematical framework in the study of Cauchy problems for parabolic equations with rough coefficients\.

**原始英文摘要**

This paper studies global existence for the Cauchy problem of nonlinear Klein-Gordon equations in three space dimensions, strictly within the regularity regime of classical local existence\. We prove it via higher-order and lower-order energy estimates\. The proof relies on two key ingredients\. The first is due to the work of Georgiev and Popivanov, which reduces quadratic nonlinearities to cubic terms plus ghost-energy-controllable terms, securing lower-order estimates\. The second is a sharp Klainerman-Sobolev-type inequality without the scaling operator, established herein, which yields enough time decay for derivatives up to second order; integration by parts then controls derivative loss terms in the higher-order estimates\.

**原始英文摘要**

We study the simplest parabolic-elliptic model of chemotaxis in $\{\\bf R^N\}$, and show the optimal criteria for the existence of infinite time blow-up of solutions with the initial data below the Chandrasekhar singular solution when $N \\geq 10$\. In particular, we exhibit the differences of the criteria between the cases $N \\geq 11$ and $N = 10$\. Our argument is based on the study of the Cauchy problem for the transformed equation involving the averaged mass of the solution and the asymptotic expansions of solutions to the Liouville equation\.

**原始英文摘要**

This paper concerns the grow-up rate of solutions to a semilinear heat equation in the unit ball with the exponential nonlinearity and an inhomogeneous term $f$\. When $f=0$, it is known that the large-time behavior of a solution changes at the critical dimension $N=10$, and the grow-up phenomenon occurs for the case $N\\ge 10$\. For the inhomogeneous case with $N\\ge 10$, the present author and a coauthor showed in \[12\] that the grow-up phenomenon disappears once $f$ exceeds a threshold\. In this paper, we provide a quantitative characterization of the disappearance of the grow-up phenomenon by obtaining the grow-up rate in the critical dimension\. Our result shows that a qualitatively different type of grow-up behavior occurs in the critical dimension compared with the case $N\\ge 11$ studied in \[12\]\. The difference is caused by a change in the outer behavior of the solution\. Even in the case $f=0$, our result is new in that it provides a rigorous justification of the formal computation by Galaktionov and King \[10\]\.

**原始英文摘要**

To describe the evolution of grain boundaries based on the underlying microscopic mechanisms of line defects \(disconnections\) and the integrated effects of a diverse range of thermodynamic driving forces, Zhang, et al\. in 2017 formulated a continuum equation\. We prove the global-in-time existence, uniqueness, and regularity of the weak solution to an initial-boundary value problem for this model\. The existence, uniqueness of the stationary solution are also established\. Finally, we investigate the large-time behavior of the weak solution of the evolution problem, and show that the solution converges to the stationary solution in a suitable sense\. Numerical simulations are carried out to validate the analysis results\. The main difficulties in the proof of main theorems are due to a non-local term with singularity, a non-smooth coefficient of the highest derivative associated with the gradient of the unknown, and the special form of free energy which is not uniformly bounded from below\. The key ingredients in the proof are the energy method, an estimate for a singular integral of the Hilbert type, Fourier transform of Hilbert transformations, and an estimate with a weight, for time-derivative of the unknown\.

**原始英文摘要**

We study a quadrature surface free boundary problem on a smooth compact finite-dimensional Riemannian manifold $\(M,g\)$\. The problem is formulated as a shape optimization problem involving a Dirichlet problem for the Laplace--Beltrami operator, with a geometric condition on the free boundary\. Under suitable uniform geometric assumptions on the admissible class, we establish its compactness and prove the stability of the corresponding Dirichlet problems, including strong convergence of the associated states in $H\_0^1\(M\)$\. We derive the first-order optimality condition for the associated shape functional\. Finally, we establish a Riemannian comparison principle for the second fundamental forms and the mean curvatures of tangent boundaries at a contact point\. These results provide a rigorous extension of the quadrature surface free boundary framework from the Euclidean setting to compact Riemannian manifolds\.

#### Ultracontractivity of heat semigroups with non-local Robin boundary conditions via two-sided bounds for a positive eigenfunction

- **作者：** Christoph Schwerdt
- **arXiv：** [2608\.30763](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30763) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.30763)
- **分类：** math\.AP、math\.FA

**完成的工作**

论文研究带边界算子的椭圆半群 L1-to-L2 估计，并构造正特征函数及其上下界。

**使用技术**

- 半群 domination
- L1-L2 smoothing
- duality
- H1 估计
- Moser 迭代

**可能的突破**

Theorem 3\.1 给出半群主估计，Section 6 证明正特征函数的上、下界。

**限制与不确定性**

依赖形式闭性、边界算子有界性和域的椭圆正则性。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** Theorem 3\.1 建立 $e^\{-tL\(A,B\)\}$ 的 L1-to-L2 估计。
   - **路线：** 第5节先证明 L1 bound，再利用半群分解和对偶性得到 L1→L2 smoothing。
2. **主张：** Section 6\.2 构造 L\(A,−C\) 的正特征函数。
   - **路线：** 用 Krein–Rutman 型正性和截断近似得到正本征函数 $\\phi $，再以椭圆方程建立初始 H1 控制。
3. **主张：** Sections 6\.3–6\.4 通过迭代完成 $\\phi $ 的上下界。
   - **路线：** 对 $\\phi $ 的幂使用 H1 估计并迭代到 $L\\infty $，再反向使用测试函数和 Harnack/比较结构得到下界。

已核查未见 AI 披露。

检查范围：缓存全文 2608\.30763\.txt；PDF 末尾区域与全文关键词检索。

**原始英文摘要**

We study heat semigroups on bounded Lipschitz domains $Ω\\subset \\mathbb\{R\}^\{d\}$ with dimension $d&gt;2$ under non-local Robin boundary conditions\. The boundary operator $B \\in \\mathcal\{L\}\( \\mathrm\{L\}^\{2\}\(\\partialΩ\)\)$ is allowed to destroy the positivity of the semigroup\. We assume that there exists a positive operator $C \\in \\mathcal\{L\}\(\\mathrm\{L\}^\{2\}\(\\partialΩ\)\)$ such that $$ \|Bu\| \\leq C\|u\| \\quad\\text\{for every \}u\\in \\mathrm\{L\}^\{2\}\(\\partialΩ\), \\qquad C\{\\bf 1\}\\in \\mathrm\{L\}^\{\\infty\}\(\\partialΩ\)\. $$ Under this assumption, we prove that the semigroup generated by the corresponding uniformly elliptic operator is ultracontractive\. More precisely, its norm from $\\mathrm\{L\}^\{2\}\(Ω\)$ to $\\mathrm\{L\}^\{\\infty\}\(Ω\)$ has short-time order $t^\{-d/4\}$\. The main step is the construction of a positive eigenfunction $φ$ of a dominating elliptic operator such that $$ 0 \\ &lt; \\ δ\\ \\leq \\ φ\\ \\leq \\ M $$ almost everywhere in $Ω$\. The upper bound is obtained by power truncations and an iteration of Sobolev exponents\. The lower bound follows by comparison with the Neumann semigroup\. The two-sided estimate gives an $\\mathrm\{L\}^\{\\infty\}$-bound for the comparison semigroup\. Nash's inequality and duality then yield ultracontractivity\.

- **arXiv：** [2608\.29484](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29484) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.29484)

   - **路线：** 将正测度集上的二阶控制回代原解，结合连续性/比较原理排除奇异集合，完成主正则性结论。

We prove a Bernstein theorem for $Φ$-anisotropic minimal hypersurfaces in dimensions $1\\leq n\\leq 7$ that the only entire smooth solutions $u$ of $Φ$-anisotropic minimal hypersurfaces equation are affine functions provided the anisotropic area functional integrand $Φ$ is sufficiently $C^\{2\}$--close to the Euclidean area integrand\. #### A Tensor Neural Network Method for High-Order Homogenization of Locally Periodic Elliptic Problems

- **作者：** Huaijia Zhang、Haochen Liu、Xia Ji、Hehu Xie
- **arXiv：** [2608\.29176](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29176) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.29176)
- **分类：** math\.NA、math\.AP
- **进展类型：** 高阶均匀化误差估计
- **阅读优先级：** 64/100 · 中优先级
- **分析深度：** 已补读正文关键部分

**完成的工作**

论文处理系数同时依赖慢变量和快周期变量的局部周期椭圆问题。作者递归构造参数化 cell problems 和宏观 correctors，证明部分两尺度展开的残差恒等式与零均值振荡的 $H^-1$ 小量，进而在无边界层的周期或精确匹配设置下得到高阶 $H^1$ 收敛；张量神经网络和确定性张量求积用于数值求解 corrector 链。

**使用技术**

- 局部周期两尺度展开
- 递归 cell problem 与宏观 corrector
- 零均值振荡的 $H^-1$ 估计
- 椭圆 coercivity 能量法与张量求积

**可能的突破**

论文把慢变量参数化的高阶 cell hierarchy、残差的零均值结构和无边界层条件连接成可计算的 $H^1$ 误差定理，使高阶局部周期均匀化能够由张量神经网络逐级实现并保持理论收敛阶。

**限制与不确定性**

$H^1$ 定理要求周期域或每个截断展开精确匹配边界、足够高的参数化 Schauder 正则性和一致椭圆性；一般 Dirichlet 边界层不在定理范围内，数值 $L^2$ 斜率被作者明确标为经验现象而非该 $H^1$ 定理的直接推论。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** Lemmas 2\.1、2\.2 和 2\.4 保证递归 cell problems 可解，并使各阶 corrector 具有后续残差估计所需的正则性。
   - **路线：** 对每个 cell 方程在快变量上积分，利用前一阶宏观方程使右端平均为零；再把 auxiliary corrector 与宏观 corrector 相加得到级联恒等式，最后对慢变量逐阶微分并反复使用参数化周期 Schauder 估计。
2. **主张：** Lemma 2\.3 把快变量上的零均值振荡转化为物理空间 $H^-1$ 中的 $O\(\\epsilon \)$ 小量。
   - **路线：** 对零均值周期函数解参数化 Poisson 方程，取其快变量梯度作为周期通量，再对复合函数作物理散度分解；与 $H^1$ 测试函数分部积分后$，\\epsilon $ 因子乘上通量的有界范数。
3. **主张：** Lemma 2\.5 给出部分展开 Uk\* 的精确残差分解，并证明主残差 Rk 在快变量上的平均为零。
   - **路线：** 把算子分解 L0、L1、L2 代入截断展开并收集 $\\epsilon $ 的幂次；Lemma 2\.2 消去直到 $\\epsilon ^\(k-2\)$ 的项，剩余项分成 $\\epsilon ^\(k-1\)Rk$ 和 $\\epsilon ^kSk$，而下一阶 cell 方程把 Rk 写成 −L0 û\{k\+1\}。
4. **主张：** Theorem 2\.1 和 Corollary 2\.1 将残差结构闭合为部分及完整展开的 $O\(\\epsilon ^k\)$ $H^1$ 误差。
   - **路线：** 对误差 $e\\epsilon k=u\\epsilon -Uk\*$ 写弱方程，使用 Lemma 2\.5；Rk 项由 Lemma 2\.3 再获得一个 $\\epsilon ，Sk$ 项由 $L\\infty $ 界和 Poincare 控制，最后以 Uk\* 的统一椭圆性在测试函数取 $e\\epsilon k$ 时吸收梯度并得到估计，三角不等式补回 $\\epsilon ^k$ u~k。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦椭圆与抛物方程，主要进展属于近似与数值分析；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**AI 声明核查**

已核查未见 AI 披露。

检查范围：PDF 全文第1–36页；缓存全文 2608\.29176\.txt。

**原始英文摘要**

We develop a high-order tensor neural network \(TNN\) method for locally periodic elliptic multiscale problems of the form $-\\nabla\\cdot\(A\(x,x/\\varepsilon\)\\nabla u\_\\varepsilon\)=f$\. Because the coefficient depends on both the slow variable $x$ and the fast periodic variable $y=x/\\varepsilon$, the high-order cell problems and macroscopic corrector equations are more involved than in the classical case $A=A\(y\)$, and the correctors depend parametrically on $x$\. We derive a computable high-order two-scale expansion and prove an $H^1$ convergence estimate for the partial expansion in boundary-layer-free settings, including periodic domains and ideal boundary-matching configurations\. The proof uses the recursive compatibility structure of the corrector hierarchy and a zero-mean oscillation estimate in $H^\{-1\}$\. We then construct a TNN framework for the high-dimensional corrector problems\. Its tensor-product structure permits deterministic one-dimensional quadrature for the cell problems, homogenized coefficients, macroscopic source terms, and loss functions, avoiding Monte Carlo integration error\. The numerical realization assumes that the coefficient entries and assembled data admit finite or controlled tensor-product representations; this computational assumption is separate from the general matrix-valued coefficient class used in the analysis\. Experiments with scalar locally periodic coefficients show accurate high-order correctors\. The $H^1$ semi-norm errors are consistent with the proved estimate, while point-normalized $L^2$ errors display the nominal high-order behavior predicted by the formal expansion\.

论文研究有界势 Engelbert–Schmidt 一维扩散的自然尺度$、L^\{2\}$ 半群和正时间正则性。作者先将速度测度与边界行为编码进对称 Dirichlet 形式，再用谱演算证明半群进入生成元的高阶域；有界势通过形式扰动加入，最终把自然尺度下的绝对连续性和边界迹恢复为 Theorem 3\.2–3\.5 的正则性结论。

**可能的突破**

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** Lemma 2\.1 把自然尺度坐标中的绝对连续性与 form-domain 边界条件转成生成元的算子域正则性。
   - **路线：** 令 s 为尺度函数、µ 为速度测度，把扩散形式写成 s 坐标下的加权一维能量；对形式域中的函数使用局部 AC 和弱导数表示，得到生成元作用后仍满足相应边界通量条件。
2. **主张：** Lemma 2\.2 证明正时间半群把 $L^\{2\}$ 初值送入任意有限阶生成元域。
   - **路线：** 利用自伴非正算子 L 的谱测度，估计 $sup\_\{\\lambda \\in \\sigma \(L\)\}\|\\lambda \|^k$ $e^\{t\\lambda \}$；该指数衰减给出 $e^\{tL\}f\\in Dom\(L^k\)$，再把结论用于带初值的演化方程。
3. **主张：** Proposition 3\.1 在有界势下构造强连续 $L^\{2\}$ 半群，并保留 Engelbert–Schmidt 形式的控制。
   - **路线：** 将乘法势作为有界形式扰动加入基准 Dirichlet 形式，闭形式表示定理给出自伴算子 B；谱下界和 Feynman–Kac 表示随后提供半群有界性及强连续性。
4. **主张：** Theorem 3\.2、Propositions 3\.4–3\.5 将算子域平滑提升为内点 $C^\{1,2\}$ 与边界条件结论。
   - **路线：** 对 $v\_t=e^\{tB\}Uf$ 使用 Lemma 2\.2 的域正则性，再以自然尺度的 AC 表示识别空间导数；canonical form domain 给出边界行为，非零时间无关 Dirichlet 数据则先减去静态 $\\xi $ 再应用同一估计。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦椭圆与抛物方程，主要进展属于正则性；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**原始英文摘要**

Let $X$ be a time-homogeneous one-dimensional diffusion whose coefficients satisfy the Engelbert--Schmidt conditions, and let $q$ be a bounded potential\. We show that the associated Feynman--Kac semigroup is smooth in time and $C^1$ in space, with a locally absolutely continuous first spatial derivative; the Kolmogorov equation holds almost everywhere\. If the drift, second-order coefficient, and potential are continuous, the Feynman--Kac value is $C^\{1,2\}$ in the interior\. Thus, in the time-homogeneous one-dimensional setting, continuity suffices for interior $C^\{1,2\}$ regularity\. We identify the corresponding semigroups with the killed and reflected Feynman--Kac functionals, treat nonzero, time-independent Dirichlet data, and give examples showing the sharpness of the continuity assumptions and the failure of the corresponding statement in two dimensions\.

#### Absence of the Lavrentiev phenomenon for a general class of parabolic double phase problems

- **作者：** Youngchae Kim、Jehan Oh
- **arXiv：** [2608\.29085](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29085) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.29085)
- **分类：** math\.AP
- **进展类型：** $theorem\_proof\_reviewed$
- **阅读优先级：** 61/100 · 中优先级
- **分析深度：** 已补读正文关键部分

**完成的工作**

论文研究具有 Orlicz 增长的抛物双相泛函 $P\(w\)=∫\[G\(\|Dw\|\)\+a\(z\)H\(\|Dw\|\)\]$ 中是否出现 Lavrentiev 间隙。正文第 1 节提出 G、H 的 $\\Delta \_\{2\}/\\nabla 2、G≺H$ 和系数模连续性条件，第 2 节建立 Young 函数复合与抛物平滑工具，第 3 节完成局部平滑逼近。Theorem 1\.1 说明有限能量映射可在任意紧内柱体 Q 中由光滑序列逼近，且函数、梯度模块和泛函能量分别收敛。

**使用技术**

- Orlicz Young 函数的 $\\Delta \_\{2\}/\\nabla 2$ 伸缩
- 抛物尺度 mollification
- 双相系数振荡的模估计
- 截断与 modular 收敛
- 局部对角逼近

**可能的突破**

核心结果把幂次双相理论的无 Lavrentiev 结论推广到一般 Young 函数，并把 G/H 的增长间隙与 a 的抛物连续模写成三个可检验的平衡条件；逼近不只保持函数收敛，也保持 Orlicz 梯度模块和原泛函能量。

**限制与不确定性**

结果是局部结论，只对有限能量的 $W^\{1,1\}$ 映射和紧内柱体成立；需要 G,H 同时满足 $\\Delta \_\{2\}/\\nabla 2、G≺H、a$ 非负连续及定理 \(1\.2\)–\(1\.4\) 中至少一个平衡条件，并未给出边界保持或无条件的全域光滑密度。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** $\\Delta \_\{2\}、\\nabla 2$ 与 G≺H 使复合增长 $\\Phi =H∘G^\{-1\}$ 具有可控伸缩，且能量有限性确定自然的 Orlicz 梯度空间。
   - **路线：** 先用 Definition 2\.1 的两侧伸缩不等式控制 Young 函数在大、小尺度的变化，再由 Lemma 2\.4–2\.5 证明复合函数的 $\\Delta \_\{2\}$ 和比值估计；这一步把后续 mollification 产生的尺度因子转成可积模块。
2. **主张：** 抛物平滑后的函数仍能在局部柱体内控制双相能量，系数 a 的振荡只产生由 $\\omega \(\\rho \)$ 衡量的误差。
   - **路线：** 在内部柱体先按空间半径 $\\rho $、时间半径 $\\rho ^\{2\}$ 作抛物卷积；把 $a\(z\)-a\(\\zeta \)$ 从卷积核中提出，用其连续模乘以 H 的伸缩估计，随后用 G≺H 将高增长项吸收到条件 \(1\.2\)、\(1\.3\) 或 \(1\.4\) 的比值中。
3. **主张：** 截断平滑序列在 L1、LG 梯度模块和 P 中分别收敛到 w、Dw 和原能量。
   - **路线：** 先对 w 和 Dw 做局部截断，避免卷积在大梯度尾部失控；用 Lemma 2\.5 的复合增长比较得到均匀可积，再由 Orlicz modular 收敛把逐点收敛提升为积分收敛，并单独估计 aH 项。
4. **主张：** 对任意 $Q\\subset \\subset \\Omega T$ 存在 $C\\infty \(Q\)$ 序列 wm，使 wm→w、Dwm→Dw 且 P\(wm,Q\)→P\(w,Q\)，从而无 Lavrentiev 现象。
   - **路线：** 将内部尺度逐级选小，使每一级的函数、梯度和双相能量误差小于 $2^\{-m\}$；把各局部近似按有限覆盖拼接，再用对角选择得到单一序列，三种条件分别对应三种尾项吸收方式。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦椭圆与抛物方程，主要进展属于分析结果；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**AI 声明核查**

检查范围：PDF 全文逐页核查，包含引言、证明章节与末尾致谢/声明区域。

**原始英文摘要**

In this paper, we prove the absence of the Lavrentiev phenomenon for a general class of parabolic double phase functionals with Orlicz growth\. The energy density is given by $$ G\(\|Dw\|\)\+a\(x,t\)H\(\|Dw\|\), $$ where $G$ and $H$ are Young functions satisfying the $Δ\_2$ and $\\nabla\_2$ conditions with $G\\prec H$, and $a\(\\cdot\)$ is a continuous nonnegative coefficient\. Under suitable balance conditions between the growth gap of $G$ and $H$ and the modulus of continuity of $a\(\\cdot\)$, we show that every finite-energy map can be approximated locally by smooth functions without loss of energy\. The result extends the known parabolic double phase theory from power type growth to a broad Young function framework and identifies the natural space-time Orlicz energy class for the problem\.

#### Apriori Estimates and Nonexistence Results for Some Quasilinear Problems in a Half-Space

- **作者：** Evgeny Galakhov、Olga Salieva
- **arXiv：** [2608\.29155](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29155) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.29155)
- **分类：** math\.AP
- **进展类型：** $theorem\_proof\_reviewed$
- **阅读优先级：** 61/100 · 中优先级
- **分析深度：** 已补读正文关键部分

**完成的工作**

论文研究半空间中 div $A\(x,u,\\nabla u\)\\ge f\(u\)$ 的非负解。正文先从局部 Lipschitz 控制和水平超平面的 infimum 构造边界 a priori $L\\infty $ 估计，再分别处理 Dirichlet 情形、容量型衰减和零极限情形，得到 Theorems 2–4 的 Liouville 非存在范围。

**使用技术**

- 弱 Harnack/最大值
- 水平超平面序列
- 截断测试
- 临界指数比较

**可能的突破**

Theorem 1 将半空间梯度/通量控制转成可用于多个非存在结论的统一增长界；Theorem 3 则绕开该界，直接以容量增长排除更宽的指数区间。

**限制与不确定性**

结论要求 A 满足 \(hf\) 结构、u 非负并满足文中的边界/增长条件；Theorem 4 还要求 f\(t\) 在无穷处具有零极限，未覆盖任意非单调或符号变号的非线性。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** Theorem 1 建立半空间非负 supersolution 的 a priori $L\\infty $ 梯度界。
   - **路线：** 在球 $B\_R\(x\*\)$ 内令 $a=\|A\(x,u,\\nabla u\)\|$，用 A 的椭圆结构控制通量，再沿水平截面比较 u\(x\)−u\(y\)；选择 R 平衡 Lipschitz 项与 f\(u\) 的增长，得到与 R 和 sup u 相关的幂次界。
2. **主张：** Corollary 1 与 Theorem 2 将边界条件转成模型 p-Laplacian 的非存在阈值。
   - **路线：** 对 Dirichlet 数据取 $c2=0，Theorem$ 1 的边界项消失；在 $f\(t\)=t^q、A=\|\\xi \|^\{p-2\}\\xi $ 时平衡半径和幂次，代入已有有界解 Liouville 结论，排除 q 落在临界区间内的非平凡解。
3. **主张：** Theorem 3 在不使用 Theorem 1 的情况下，以容量增长排除 q&gt;p−1 的非负非平凡解。
   - **路线：** 作者假设解存在并在平移半空间中选取水平面序列，利用 Lemma 1 的加权积分增长估计控制 $v^\\gamma $ 的质量；该增长与 \(hf\) 给出的截断测试不等式矛盾。
4. **主张：** Lemma 2 与 Theorem 4 处理 f 在无穷处趋零的边界情形并完成最后的非存在结论。
   - **路线：** 先由每个水平面 inf $u=0$ 的假设选取局部衰减点，再用重标度把这些点送到固定球；增长 lemma 给出 $1-\\epsilon $ 次幂的上界，随后将零极限和 \(hf\) 代回，排除剩余解。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦椭圆与抛物方程，主要进展属于分析结果；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

#### Gradient Estimates Near the Natural Exponent for Very Weak Solutions to $A\_p$-Weighted Quasilinear Elliptic Equations

- **作者：** Sun-Sig Byun、Minkyu Lim
- **arXiv：** [2608\.30155](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30155) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.30155)
- **分类：** math\.AP
- **进展类型：** $theorem\_proof\_reviewed$
- **阅读优先级：** 61/100 · 中优先级
- **分析深度：** 已补读正文关键部分

**完成的工作**

论文研究带矩阵退化权 M 的 Ap 加权拟线性椭圆方程，在 very weak 解、初始梯度可低于自然指数 p 的情况下建立靠近 p 的 Calderón–Zygmund 梯度估计。正文第 2 节定义矩阵算子$、\\omega =\|M\|^p、very$ weak 解并陈述 Theorem 2\.2；第 3 节准备 Ap 权与 Lipschitz truncation，第 4 节先证明高可积性和比较解存在，第 5 节以齐次比较问题和加权 level-set $good-\\lambda $ 迭代完成主定理。结论是 $f\\in L^\\gamma \_\\omega 、\\gamma \\in \[p-\\delta \_\{0\},p\+\\delta \_\{0\}\]$ 时 $Du\\in L^\\gamma \_\\omega $。

**使用技术**

- 矩阵权与标量 Ap 约化
- $V\_p$ 单调性
- 加权 Lipschitz truncation
- 齐次比较解
- level-set 覆盖与 $good-\\lambda $ 迭代

**可能的突破**

新意在于不要求 M 有统一上下界或小 BMO 正则，而仅用有界 condition number 和 $\\omega \\in Ap$；通过矩阵结构、加权高可积性及 Lipschitz truncation 的联结，把 very weak 的低于 p 初始正则性提升到 p 邻域的完整梯度可积性。

**限制与不确定性**

$\\delta \_\{0\}$ 只依赖 $n,p,\\nu ,L$、矩阵 condition-number 界和 $\[\\omega \]Ap$；估计是局部球上、对满足结构条件的 very weak 解成立，矩阵 M 被假设可延拓到全空间以规避域外延拓技术，不能据此覆盖任意非 Ap 权或无单调性的算子。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** 矩阵算子 AM 在 $\\omega =\|M\|^p$ 与有界 condition number 下满足加权 p-growth 和 $V\_p$ 强单调性。
   - **路线：** 由 $\|M\|\|M^\{-1\}\|\\le \\Lambda $ 比较 $\|M\\xi \|$ 与 $\|M\|\|\\xi \|$，将矩阵退化量压缩为标量权 $\\omega $；再把 $A\(x,M\\xi \)$ 的单调性写成 $V\(M\\xi \)$ 差的平方，得到\(2\.1\)–\(2\.3\)所需的 coercivity。
2. **主张：** 加权 Ap 自改善和局部比较工具给出齐次解的 higher-integrability 控制。
   - **路线：** 先用 Ap 的 doubling、最大函数有界性和 reverse Hölder 选出 $\\sigma &gt;0；Section$ 4 的 Lemmas 4\.1–4\.2 对齐次比较解应用加权 Caccioppoli/Gehring，随后 Corollary 4\.3 通过存在性构造具有 $p\+\\sigma $ 控制的 w。
3. **主张：** 对 u 与齐次比较解 w 的差 v，Lipschitz truncation 产生低于 p 的比较估计。
   - **路线：** 在 $B\_\{2r\}$ 中取 $w=u\+W^\{1,p-\\delta \_\{0\}\}\_\{\\omega ,0\}$ 的齐次解，令 $v=u-w$ 并用 Lemma 3\.4 的 $v\_\\lambda $ 测试；在非好集合 $E\_\\lambda $ 与其补集分别使用单调性、最大函数和 Young 不等式，按 $\\lambda ^\{-\(1\+\\delta \)\}$ 积分得到\(5\.3\)的 $V\_p$ 差估计。
4. **主张：** level-set 覆盖、加权 Chebyshev 与 $good-\\lambda $ 迭代把比较估计提升为 Theorem 2\.2 的所有 $\\gamma \\in \[p-\\delta \_\{0\},p\+\\delta \_\{0\}\]$ 梯度界。
   - **路线：** 对 \|Du\| 的高水平集用 Vitali 球覆盖，套用 Lemma 5\.1 获得\(5\.12\)–\(5\.14\)的递推；Fubini 把层积分转换为截断的 $L^\\gamma $ 模块，选 T 大$、\\epsilon $ 小使递推系数小于一，再令截断层 $t\\to \\infty $并用权重 doubling 完成\(5\.15\)–\(5\.17\)及主估计。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦椭圆与抛物方程，主要进展属于分析结果；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**原始英文摘要**

We establish local Calderón--Zygmund estimates near the natural exponent for very weak solutions to matrix-weighted quasilinear equations\\\[ - \\mathrm\{div\\,\} A\_\{\\mathbb\{M\}\}\(x,Du\) = - \\mathrm\{div\\,\} A\_\{\\mathbb\{M\}\}\(x,\\mathbf\{f\}\), \\qquad A\_\{\\mathbb\{M\}\}\(x,ξ\)=\\mathbb\{M\}\(x\)A\(x,\\mathbb\{M\}\(x\)ξ\), \\\] where $A$ has $p$-growth and strong monotonicity, and $\\mathbb\{M\}$ is a measurable positive-definite matrix field\. We assume that $\\mathbb\{M\}$ has bounded condition number and that \\\(ω:=\|\\mathbb\{M\}\|^p\\in A\_p\\\), without imposing uniform upper or lower bounds on $\\mathbb\{M\}$\. This extends the near-natural Calderón--Zygmund theory developed by Adimurthi--Phuc \\cite\{AP15\} to the matrix-degenerate setting: There exists $δ\_0&gt;0$ such that every very weak solution $u \\in W^\{p-δ\_\{0\}\}\_\{ω, \\mathrm\{loc\}\}$ satisfies \\\[ \\mathbf\{f\}\\in L^γ\_\{ω,\\mathrm\{loc\}\} \\Longrightarrow Du\\in L^γ\_\{ω,\\mathrm\{loc\}\} \\\] for $p-δ\_0\\leγ\\le p\+δ\_0$\. The proof requires handling the lack of energy estimates below the natural exponent and the use of Lipschitz truncation in the weighted setting\. We achieve this through comparison estimates, higher integrability, and weighted analysis techniques\.

#### Critical Morrey Rigidity and Removable Singularities for Five-Dimensional Stationary Navier-Stokes Flows

- **作者：** Yubo Chen、Wendong Wang、Xiao Wang、Guoxu Yang、Jianbo Yu
- **arXiv：** [2608\.29664](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29664) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.29664)
- **分类：** math\.AP
- **进展类型：** 偏微分方程正则性

**完成的工作**

研究五维和四维 Navier–Stokes 型 suitable weak solutions 的速度/压力正则性、尺度不变流刚性及 head-pressure 符号机制。

**使用技术**

- 压力重构
- Morrey 估计
- 尺度不变 blow-up
- 单调通量
- $\\epsilon -$正则性

**可能的突破**

Theorems 1\.1、1\.3、1\.4 和 1\.11 将局部正则性归结为全局尺度不变流的符号刚性。

**限制与不确定性**

维数固定为四或五维，依赖 suitable weak solution、尺度不变界和局部几何假设。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** Propositions 1\.9–1\.10 建立全局尺度不变流的 head-pressure 符号刚性。
   - **路线：** Section 2 先重构压力并建立 Morrey 紧性，再用径向单调恒等式和 zero-defect tangents 消除正 head pressure。
2. **主张：** Theorem 1\.4 给出五维 velocity-only rigidity。
   - **路线：** Section 4 将符号刚性与尺度不变通量结合，排除非平凡全局切片。
3. **主张：** Theorems 1\.1 和 1\.3 由 blow-up 与 $\\epsilon -$正则性闭合。
   - **路线：** Section 5 将局部假设违背转化为尺度不变 blow-up，应用 Theorem 1\.4/Proposition 1\.10 后得到矛盾。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦流体方程，主要进展属于奇性与适定性；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**AI 声明核查**

检查范围：PDF 末尾 Acknowledgments/Appendix A 区域及全文关键词检索。

**原始英文摘要**

We prove a critical Morrey rigidity theorem for the five-dimensional stationary Navier--Stokes equations\. More precisely, every smooth solution on $\\mathbb R^5\\setminus\\\{0\\\}$ satisfying \\\[ \\sup\_\{R&gt;0\}R^\{-2\}\\int\_\{B\_R\}\|u\|^3\\,dx&lt;\\infty \\\] is identically zero, up to an additive constant in the pressure\. This replaces the pointwise Type-I control in the known higher-dimensional rigidity theory by a velocity-only, scale-invariant averaged condition that allows spatial concentration\. The proof develops a weak head-pressure mechanism that does not rely on pointwise pressure estimates or classical normal traces\. We reconstruct a canonical pressure from the velocity, derive a renormalized inequality for the positive head pressure, and introduce two monotone radial fluxes\. Annular energy estimates, suitable-weak compactness, and blow-up and blow-down limits are then used to identify the endpoint fluxes and force rigidity\. As an application, we obtain a removable-singularity criterion in dimension five: if a suitable weak solution is smooth away from one point and either its scale-invariant Dirichlet energy or its cubic velocity Morrey quantity remains bounded near that point, then the singularity is removable\. Thus, within the isolated-singularity class, the smallness assumption in the classical stationary regularity criterion is replaced by boundedness\. We also prove the corresponding velocity-only cubic Morrey rigidity theorem in dimension four by a different finite-energy argument\.

#### Finite-energy spacetimes with torus symmetry\. Cauchy stability of Einstein-Euler and Einstein-Navier-Stokes areal flows

- **作者：** Bruno Le Floch、Philippe G\. LeFloch
- **arXiv：** [2608\.31171](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31171) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.31171)

**完成的工作**

研究具有环面对称的 Einstein–matter 时空，建立 areal 流、物质耦合系统、守恒/熵不等式及 Einstein–Navier–Stokes 型模型。

**使用技术**

- 对称约化
- areal 坐标
- 守恒流
- 未来凸性
- 数学熵与总变差

**可能的突破**

Theorem 1\.1 给出 Einstein areal flow 的 JKL 形式，后续章节构造与比较 Euler、Navier–Stokes 和耗散模型的统一势函数框架。

**限制与不确定性**

结论限于环面等距对称、给定压力律和正性/凸性假设；后半部分包含模型提议和结构分析，并非单一存在性定理。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** Theorem 1\.1 将 Einstein areal flow 写成 JKL 形式。
   - **路线：** Section 1\.2 从 Einstein–matter 方程提取一阶变量和约束，利用 areal 坐标整理为稳定的双曲系统。
2. **主张：** Section 4 建立质量能量/粒子数流和数学熵不等式。
   - **路线：** 构造守恒流，证明参考流的未来凸性，再将凸性转化为熵耗散不等式。
3. **主张：** Section 7–8 将结构应用于 Einstein–Euler 和 Einstein–Navier–Stokes areal flows。
   - **路线：** 把耗散应力和粒子产生项代入约化系统，利用单调性、总变差和 timelike integrals 推出模型的结构后果。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦流体方程，主要进展属于稳定性；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**AI 声明核查**

已核查未见 AI 披露。

检查范围：PDF 末尾 Acknowledgments/References 区域及全文关键词检索。

**原始英文摘要**

We establish finite-energy Cauchy stability for vacuum and matter spacetimes with $T^2$ symmetry on $T^3$, under general constitutive equations satisfying hyperbolicity and mild asymptotic conditions at vacuum and large mass-energy density\. First, using the area of the symmetry orbits as a time function, we introduce the JKL formulation of Einstein areal flows\}, a first-order evolution-constraint system coupling a wave-map structure for the geometry to hyperbolic matter balance laws and weighted transport equations for the twists and the momentum tangent to the symmetry orbits\. Second, we also define two classes of hyperbolic Einstein-Navier-Stokes models, constructed from \(as we call them\) a Navier-Stokes potential and a relaxation rate map\. The proposed particle-production model has divergence-free matter stress and non-negative particle-number production, while the proposed dissipated-energy model conserves particle number and dissipates fluid mass-energy, while an auxiliary stress restores the total stress-energy conservation required by the contracted Bianchi identity\. For regular finite-energy \(Einstein, Euler, Navier-Stokes\) flows, we establish maximum and invariant-domain principles, geometric and energy monotonicity formulas, spacelike and timelike energy estimates, and total-variation estimates\. Third, together, these estimates yield finite-energy Cauchy stability in the future expanding regime and, in the contracting regime, until the spacelike volume collapses\. They are independent of the viscosity \(or relaxation\) mechanisms and cover vacuum, unbounded mass-energy density, and arbitrary finite rapidity\. They also extend to weakly regular Einstein-Euler areal flows satisfying a reference mathematical entropy inequality\.

**完成的工作**

研究狭长 slab 中稳态 Navier–Stokes 流，构造 Poiseuille 基流上的广义弱解并证明衰减与小力唯一性。

   - **路线：** 结合局部 Stokes 正则性与轴向加权能量估计，逐层控制速度和压力。
3. **主张：** Proposition 4\.1 证明小外力下唯一性。
   - **路线：** 对差方程 $w=v-U$ 进行能量积分，小 Poiseuille 梯度使对流项可吸收，推出 $w=0$。

**原始英文摘要**

In this paper, we investigate the uniqueness and asymptotic behavior of the stationary Navier-Stokes equations in a slab domain with no-slip boundary conditions\. Specifically, under the given external force and the small Poiseuille flow assumptions, we prove that there is an H1 solution, and obtain the pointwise decay around the Poiseuille flow at far field\. Furthermore, if the force is small, the solution is shown to be unique\. The key point of the proof is the estimate of the Dirichlet integral of the solutions in the truncated domains and the Stokes regularity estimates\.

**原始英文摘要**

In this article, we present a new pointwise estimate for a weighted rough singular integral operator in the setting of stratified Lie groups\. This operator, $T\_\{Ω, \\varpi\}$, is based on a kernel $Ω$ and a weight $\\varpi$, where the kernel satisfies a natural size condition and a cancellation property with respect to the weight $\\varpi$\. Moreover, we do not assume any kind of regularity on these objects\. This weighted rough singular integral operator, applied to a function $f$, is estimated through a combination of information involving a weighted maximal function of the gradient of $f$ and a weighted Morrey space\. We also deduce from this pointwise estimate some new weighted functional inequalities and, as an application, we obtain a uniqueness result for a rough version of the stationary Navier-Stokes equation over the Heisenberg group\.

**原始英文摘要**

In this paper we consider the Navier-Stokes equations in exterior domains of $\\mathbb\{R\}^n$, $n\\geq 3$, with a periodically in time moving boundary $\\partialΩ\(t\)$ and external force $f\(t\)$\. For this case we prove the existence of a locally unique mild time periodic solution in weighted function spaces with radially symmetric Muckenhoupt weights\. The solutions split into a stationary part controlled by potential theoretic estimates and a purely oscillatory part constructed as mild solution via analytic semigroup theory\. To deal with perturbation terms of even second order - coming from a coordinate transform and the moving boundary - in weighted, homogeneous Sobolev spaces a maximal $L^1$ type regularity estimate will be used in weighted Lorentz spaces\. To control the convective term an $\\mathcal H^\\infty$-calculus in weighted spaces of the Stokes operator, its $BIP$ property and embedding estimates of fractional powers are exploited, see a recent paper by the authors: The Stokes operator on exterior domains in homogeneous weighted function spaces: From weak theory to $\\mathscr H^\\infty$-calculus to fractional domains \(2025\)\.

**原始英文摘要**

We consider the Stokes operator $A$ on smooth exterior domains $Ω$ of $\\mathbb\{R\}^n$ in homogeneous Sobolev spaces $\\widehat H^\{κ,q\}\_w\(Ω\)$ with radially symmetric Muckenhoupt weights $w\\in \\mathscr A\_q$\. A fundamental property is the existence of a bounded $\\mathscr H^\\infty$-calculus of the Stokes operator on weighted nonhomogeneous and homogeneous $L^q$ Sobolev spaces\. This property implies the existence of uniformly bounded purely imaginary powers $A^\{it\}$, $t\\in\\mathbb\{R\}$, and the characterization of domains of fractional powers $A^θ$ equipped with nonhomogeneous \($\\\| u\\\|\_\{L^q\_w\} \+ \\\|A^θu\\\|\_\{L^q\_w\}$\) as well as homogeneous norm \($\\\|A^θu\\\|\_\{L^q\_w\}$\) as complex interpolation spaces\. The final aim is the identification with homogeneous spaces $\\widehat\{\\mathcal D\}\(\(-Δ\_\{q,w\}\)^θ\) = \[L^q\_\{w\},\\widehat\{\\mathcal D\}\(-Δ\_\{q,w\}\)\]\_θ$ intersected by a space of solenoidal vector fields\. Moreover, we obtain weighted variational inequalities for weak solutions of the Stokes equations, weighted $L^q$-$L^r$ decay estimates of the Stokes semigroup and $L^p$-maximal regularity on $L^q\_\{σ,w\}\(Ω\)$\.

**原始英文摘要**

For the 3D semilinear Euler-Poisson-Darboux equation $\\square u\+\\fracμ\{t\}\\partial\_tu=\|u\|^p$, where $t\\geq1$, $μ&gt;0$ and $p&gt;1$, it is conjectured that there is a critical exponent $p\_\{crit\}\(3,μ\)=\\max\\\{p\_s\(3\+μ\), p\_f\(3\)\\\}$ with the Strauss exponent $p\_s\(3\+μ\)=\\frac\{μ\+4\+\\sqrt\{μ^2\+16μ\+32\}\}\{2\(μ\+2\)\}$ and the Fujita exponent $p\_f\(3\)=\\frac\{5\}\{3\}$ such that when $p&gt;p\_\{crit\}\(3,μ\)$, the small data solution $u$ exists globally, otherwise, when $1&lt;p\\le p\_\{crit\}\(3,μ\)$, the solution $u$ can blow up in finite time\. It is pointed out that for $1&lt;p\\le p\_\{crit\}\(3,μ\)$, the blowup of solution $u$ has been shown\. However, it is still open for the global existence of small solution $u$ when $p&gt;p\_\{crit\}\(3,μ\)$\. Note that $p\_\{crit\}\(3,μ\)=p\_s\(3\+μ\)$ for $0&lt;μ&lt;\\frac\{14\}\{5\}$ and $p\_\{crit\}\(3,μ\)=p\_f\(3\)$ for $μ\\geq\\frac\{14\}\{5\}$\. In the recent paper \[16\], the authors have obtained the global small solution $u$ for $p&gt;\\max\\\{\\frac\{5\}\{3\},1\+\\frac\{2\}μ\\\}$ and $μ\\geq\\frac\{14\}\{5\}$\. In this paper, by utilizing the hypergeometric Riemann representation and establishing some delicate pointwise spacetime weighted estimates, we prove the global existence of small data radial solution $u$ in the remaining range of $\\frac\{5\}\{3\}&lt;p\\leq1\+\\frac\{2\}μ$ and $\\frac\{14\}\{5\}\\leqμ&lt;3$\. Therefore, for the radially symmetric case and $μ\\geq\\frac\{14\}\{5\}$, the global existence problem of small data solution $u$ is solved when $p&gt;p\_\{crit\}\(3,μ\)=\\frac\{5\}\{3\}$\.

**原始英文摘要**

We prove that a point atom in an endpoint kinetic-energy measure of a three-dimensional incompressible Navier-Stokes flow on the flat torus forces quantitative concentration of the actual pressure\. At each restart time tau, a same-state constraint-response comparison evolves u\(tau\) by the pressure-free componentwise advection-diffusion equation driven by u, and subtracts the resulting passive field z\_tau\. Writing r\_tau = u - z\_tau and g\_tau = Q z\_tau, we obtain an exact relative pressure-work identity\. If the atom has mass m, Nash smoothing makes z\_tau terminally non-atomic while r\_tau retains terminal atomic mass at least m; hence, after every fixed restart, the terminal limsup of the accumulated pressure work is at least m/2\. Mesoscopic localization yields scale-explicit lower bounds for gauge-invariant L2 pressure oscillation and for the local L2 mass of the pressure gradient in shrinking terminal cylinders\. Consequently, on every terminal neighborhood of the atomic point, the pressure modulo functions of time and its gradient fail to be square integrable\. The relative pressure-work principle extends to constrained solenoidal Oseen evolutions: as tau approaches T\*, the zero-initial responses vanish in weak-tail topologies, although every fixed restart retains the same lower bound\. This identifies a necessary pressure-work mechanism, generated by the incompressibility constraint, that links endpoint atoms to local pressure concentration, without requiring a full regularity hypothesis\.

#### The flexible part of intermittent Onsager theorem for the two-dimensional stochastic incompressible Euler equations

- **作者：** Zhenxin Liu、Wenhu Zhong
- **arXiv：** [2608\.30582](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30582) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.30582)
- **分类：** math\.AP、math\.PR
- **进展类型：** 随机流体
- **阅读优先级：** 65/100 · 中优先级
- **分析深度：** 已补读正文关键部分

**完成的工作**

论文研究随机流体方程的 intermittent Onsager 现象，通过 Newton–Euler–Reynolds 与 Nash 迭代构造 martingale solutions。

**使用技术**

- Newton–Euler–Reynolds iteration
- Nash perturbation
- Reynolds stress
- martingale compactness
- 归纳估计

**可能的突破**

Theorems 1\.1–1\.2 给出随机解的 Hölder/能量异常结论。

**限制与不确定性**

依赖噪声协方差、初始正则性及迭代参数范围；构造结论是概率/鞅意义下。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** Newton–Euler–Reynolds scheme 逐步降低 Reynolds stress。
   - **路线：** Section 3 构造频率局部化扰动，解线性化 Euler–Reynolds 系统并控制速度、压力和 stress 的尺度。
2. **主张：** Nash perturbation 恢复空间正则性并控制随机误差。
   - **路线：** Section 4 对速度加入 Nash 型微振荡，利用随机积分估计和参数选择使新 stress 满足下一阶归纳界。
3. **主张：** Theorems 1\.1–1\.2 由 Section 5 归纳界和紧性闭合。
   - **路线：** 统一所有迭代误差后，用 martingale compactness 抽取极限并验证方程/能量性质，得到主定理。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦流体方程，主要进展属于解的构造；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**AI 声明核查**

**原始英文摘要**

This work is concerned with the flexible part of Onsager theorem for the two-dimensional stochastic incompressible Euler equations\. We develop a stochastic and intermittent variant of the Newton--Nash iteration scheme, which incorporates new stochastic pressure, Reynolds stress and intermittency perturbations to formalize the stochastic and intermittent fluctuations\. For any $γ\\in \\left\[ \{0,\\frac\{1\}\{3\}\} \\right\)$, we employ this iteration scheme to construct $γ$-Hölder-continuous martingale solutions for the two-dimensional stochastic incompressible Euler equations\. These martingale solutions exhibit dissipative behavior and intermittency, thereby settling the flexible part of intermittent Onsager theorem for the two-dimensional stochastic incompressible Euler equations\.

#### Refined decay estimates for global solutions to the rotating MHD equations

- **作者：** Mikihiro Fujii、Yang Li
- **arXiv：** [2608\.29794](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29794) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.29794)
- **分类：** math\.AP
- **进展类型：** $theorem\_proof\_reviewed$
- **阅读优先级：** 64/100 · 中优先级
- **分析深度：** 已补读正文关键部分

**完成的工作**

论文研究有 Coriolis 力的三维旋转不可压 MHD 方程在全空间的精细衰减。正文第 1 节在临界 $Ḣ^\{1/2\}$ 框架陈述大初值但快速旋转下的全局解 Theorem 1\.1，并给出有 L1 初值时速度/磁场的改进衰减 Theorem 1\.3；第 2 节准备 Besov 乘积、旋转半群和 Fourier splitting 工具，第 3 节分解旋转线性群，第 4 节用 Duhamel 项逐项估计速度和磁场。核心结果是速度在 $p=4$ 处带 log 修正的分段衰减，以及带一阶空间矩时额外快半阶的衰减。

**使用技术**

- 旋转 Stokes 半群 Fourier 分解
- Besov 插值与乘积估计
- Duhamel 非线性分解
- Fourier splitting/L1 热核衰减
- 速度—磁场耦合 bootstrap

**可能的突破**

贡献是把旋转半群的 dispersive 因子 $D\_p\(\|\\Omega \|t\)$ 与热扩散的不同尺度结合，在临界 $Ḣ^\{1/2\}$ 全局解上给出按 $p&lt;4、p=4、p&gt;4$ 分段的精细速度衰减，并用一阶矩条件将速度和磁场都推进到额外 1/2 阶。

**限制与不确定性**

Theorem 1\.1 需要 $\|\\Omega \|$ 足够大以相对于临界初值闭合全局解，并需无散度、L1 及 $Ḣ^\{1/2\}$ 数据；Theorem 1\.3 额外要求 $\|x\|u0、\|x\|B0\\in L1$，衰减结论针对 R3 与该旋转 MHD 结构，不能直接用于无旋转或有边界域。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** 旋转线性化方程的速度传播子分解为热半群乘以两个 Fourier 投影，磁场传播子为热半群。
   - **路线：** 对线性系统作 Fourier 变换，将 $e3\\times u$ 的反对称矩阵按 $P\_\\pm \(D\)$ 对角化，得到\(3\.2\)的 $e^\{t\\Delta \}e^\{\\pm it\\Omega D3/\|D\|\}$ 两支；磁场没有 Coriolis 项，保留 $e^\{t\\Delta \}$。
2. **主张：** 线性速度半群满足按 p 分段的 $D\_p\(\|\\Omega \|t\)$ 衰减，磁场满足热核衰减；一阶矩可换取额外时间幂。
   - **路线：** 对 dyadic 块应用旋转 Fourier 相位的振荡估计，再与 L1→Lp 热核界和 Besov 插值拼接$；p=4$ 时两侧指数相交产生 log 因子，而 $\|x\|f\\in L1$ 允许在 Fourier 原点作一阶消失展开。
3. **主张：** 速度/磁场非线性 Duhamel 项可在临界全局解范数下分别满足与线性项同阶的卷积估计。
   - **路线：** 将解写成\(4\.1\)中的 $T\_\\Omega u0、e^\{t\\Delta \}B0$ 与三个二次积分 $N\_\\Omega ^\{vel\}\[u\]、N\_\\Omega ^\{vel\}\[B\]、N^\{mag\}\[u,B\]$；用 Lemma 4\.1 处理速度—速度项，利用磁场热扩散和 Lemmas 4\.2–4\.3 处理交叉项，再以 Lemma 2\.5 积分 $D\_p$。
4. **主张：** 在 $\|\\Omega \|\\ge \\Omega \_\{0\}$ 时全局临界解的衰减满足 Theorem 1\.1，并在一阶矩条件下满足 Theorem 1\.3 的增强半阶速率。
   - **路线：** 把三类 Duhamel 界代回加权时间范数，选择 $\\Omega \_\{0\}$ 使二次项系数小于 1，bootstrap 即得速度与磁场的分段衰减；有一阶矩时使用 Lemma 3\.1\(2\) 和 Lemma 3\.3\(2\) 的增强线性界重复卷积估计。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦流体方程，主要进展属于长时间行为；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**AI 声明核查**

已核查未见 AI 披露。

检查范围：PDF 全文逐页核查，包含引言、证明章节与末尾致谢/声明区域。

**原始英文摘要**

In this paper, we consider the Cauchy problem for the incompressible magnetohydrodynamic equations with the Coriolis force in the three-dimensional whole space\. For large initial data, it is known that the Cauchy problem admits a unique global solution in critical Sobolev space $\\dot\{H\}^\{1/2\}\(\\mathbb\{R\}^3\)$ provided that the speed of rotation is fast enough\. By using delicate dispersive estimates, we show refined decay estimates of the velocity field and magnetic field\. These decay rates significantly improve the ones obtained by Kim \[\\emph\{J\. Differential Equations\.\}, 2022\] in the subcritical Sobolev framework $H^s\(\\mathbb\{R\}^3\)$ with $1/2&lt;s&lt;3/2$\.

#### A Uniqueness theorem for the Vortex-Wave system with non-constant vorticity near the point vortex

- **作者：** David Meyer
- **arXiv：** [2608\.29721](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29721) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.29721)
- **分类：** math\.AP
- **进展类型：** $theorem\_proof\_reviewed$
- **阅读优先级：** 61/100 · 中优先级
- **分析深度：** 已补读正文关键部分

**完成的工作**

论文研究带非恒定涡量的二维 vortex-wave 系统唯一性。系统由 $L\\infty $ 正则涡量 w、点涡位置 X\(t\) 和 Biot–Savart 速度组成；正文第 1 节把方程移到以 X\(t\) 为中心的坐标，第 2 节证明涡量在点涡附近的高阶平坦性和支撑传播，并构造随流调制距离 d，第 3 节用五项加权距离能量 $E\_t$ 做微分估计，附录提供 Biot–Savart 的局部 log-Lipschitz 引理。若初始涡量满足 $\|w0\(x\)-w0\(X0\)\|=O\(\|x-X0\|^\\alpha \)、\\alpha &gt;2$，则 Theorem 1\.1 给出全局唯一性。

**使用技术**

- 点涡中心坐标变换
- Biot–Savart 局部估计
- 调制距离 d 与共形流
- 加权 L2 距离泛函
- Osgood/Gronwall 型能量闭合

**可能的突破**

具体新意是允许初始涡量在点涡处非恒定，只要求超过二阶的平坦性；通过把两条流的差异同时编码进调制 stream function、径向距离和角向变量，作者把原本非 log-Lipschitz 的点涡速度转化成可积的 $E\_t$ $log\(2\+E\_t^\{-1\}\)$ 控制。

**限制与不确定性**

核心阈值是 $\\alpha &gt;2$，且证明需要 $L1\\cap L\\infty $ 初始涡量和紧支撑/支撑传播控制；作者明确指出当前方法尚未达到 $\\alpha \\ge 1$ 的 Lipschitz 数据范围，故不能推广到任意非恒定有界涡量。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** 将点涡位置移到原点后，vortex-wave 系统变为带奇异径向速度的输运方程，且 $\\alpha &gt;2$ 的平坦性沿流传播。
   - **路线：** 对每个解作 x↦x−X\(t\) 变换，得到\(1\.5\)中的 ϖ、v 和点涡项；先用 Biot–Savart 的 $L1\\cap L\\infty $ 界和径向点涡流证明流不穿过原点，再用 Lemma 2\.1 的支撑半径与局部积分估计把 $\|ϖ-ϖ\(0\)\|\\le C\|x\|^\\alpha $ 传到正时间。
2. **主张：** 调制距离 $d\(t,x\)=exp\(2\\pi \\Psi \(t,x\)\)$ 在点涡附近满足 $d\\approx \|x\|、\\nabla d\\approx \\nabla \|x\|$，并沿对应流线变化缓慢。
   - **路线：** 把规则速度的 stream function $\\Psi \_R$ 与 log\|x\| 合成 $\\Psi $；由 $\\Psi \_R$ 在原点二阶消失得到\(2\.11\)–\(2\.14\)，再使用 $ū\\cdot \\nabla d=0$ 的正交关系和时间导数估计构造近似守恒距离。
3. **主张：** 由 $d、\\Psi \_R$ 和角向差组成的距离 D 控制流图的加权位置差，并形成有限的能量 $E\_t$。
   - **路线：** 在两条流轨道位于同一尺度的区域用 cutoff $\\eta $ 拼接径向/角向调制项，在外部用欧氏差；Lemma 2\.4 将 D 下界为 $\|x\|^\{\\beta /2-1\}\|x-y\|$，随后\(2\.24\)定义沿第一初始标记流的积分 $E\_t$。
4. **主张：** $E\_t$ 满足 $dE\_t/dt\\le C$ $E\_t$ $log\(2\+E\_t^\{-1\}\)$，且相同初值使 $E\_0=0$，从而两解完全相同。
   - **路线：** 先用 Lemmas 3\.2–3\.5 分别控制速度差、调制 stream function 时间差、径向项和点涡速度；再对 $E\_t^1–E\_t^5$ 求导，利用\(3\.4\)、\(3\.10\)和 Jensen 将每个对数误差归并到同一 Osgood 函数。Osgood 积分排除从零出发的正能量，最后由输运表示恢复涡量及 X 的唯一性。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦流体方程，主要进展属于分析结果；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**AI 声明核查**

已核查未见 AI 披露。

检查范围：PDF 全文逐页核查，包含引言、证明章节与末尾致谢/声明区域。

**原始英文摘要**

We study the uniqueness of the vortex wave system, i\.e\.\\ the 2D incompressible Euler equations with vorticity consisting of a point vortex and a bounded part\. We show that if the initial vorticity and point vortex location $\(w\_0,X\_0\)$ satisfy a constraint of the type $\|w\_0\(x\)-w\_0\(X\_0\)\|=O\(\|x-X\_0\|^α\)$ for $α&gt; 2$ and the vorticity is in $L^\\infty$, then solutions are unique, globally in time, even though the velocity is not log-Lipschitz\.

#### On smooth self similar solution to compressible Euler equation with physical vacuum

- **作者：** Jihoi Kim
- **arXiv：** [2608\.30443](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30443) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.30443)
- **分类：** math\.AP
- **进展类型：** 自相似 Euler 光滑轨道
- **阅读优先级：** 61/100 · 中优先级
- **分析深度：** 已补读正文关键部分

**完成的工作**

论文在一组接近临界参数的范围内研究球对称可压缩 Euler–Poisson 型自相似常微分系统，证明从真空点 P1 出发、穿过声速点 P3 并趋于无穷远点 P4 的 holosphere 轨道可在 P3 处达到 $C\\infty $ 正则。核心是相图根分支的几何退化、P3 附近的半经典重标度、临界频率 Taylor 系数的非线性递推，以及利用参数连续性完成轨道匹配。

**使用技术**

- 声速根分支与相图几何
- P3 附近对角化及半经典重标度
- Maillet 型非线性级数控制
- 参数连续性和轨道唯一性

**可能的突破**

论文把 P3 附近的无限正则性条件压缩为显式级数系数 $S\\infty \(d,\\ell\)$ 的非退化条件，并用两侧轨道的出口方向改变证明一列参数 rn↑r\+ 上存在从真空 P1 到衰减 P4 的 $C\\infty $ holosphere 轨道。

**限制与不确定性**

定理要求 d−1&lt;ℓ&lt;d、r 接近 r\+\(d,ℓ\) 且 $S\\infty \(d,\\ell\)\\ne 0$；非零性在文中通过可收敛级数的数值研究给出特定参数证据，作者也把从光滑局部数据产生该自相似 blow-up 的动力学问题列为开放问题。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** Section 2 的根分支引理和 Lemma 3\.4 确定参数区间内 P1、P2、P3、P4 的相对位置及从 P1/P3 出发轨道的几何可行性。
   - **路线：** 直接分析 $\\Delta 、\\Delta \_\{1\}、\\Delta \_\{2\}$ 的根式和斜率符号，证明绿色/红色根分支的交汇顺序；随后在 P1、P2、P3 处线性化相图并用特征方向选出唯一的入射或出射轨道，为后续匹配固定轨道端点。
2. **主张：** Lemmas 4\.2–4\.3 把 P3 附近的退化轨道方程重写为含小参数 b 的拟线性问题，并显式产生二次抵消。
   - **路线：** 利用 r↑r\+ 时 P2、P3 的距离和两个斜率同时趋同，作对角变量变换及 $W̃、\\Sigma ̃$ 的 b 重标度；退化项的抵消把轨道方程化为 $u\(1-u\)\\Theta '-\[\\gamma -2\+\(\\nu b\+3\)u\]\\Theta =-G/b$ 的正则奇点问题。
3. **主张：** Sections 5–7 控制临界频率展开并证明在 $S\\infty \\ne 0$ 时 $C\\infty $ 轨道会从 P3 两侧离开到指定根分支。
   - **路线：** 先在 $b=0$ 极限问题中用非线性 Maillet 级数估计得到有界系数，再把 b 依赖问题共轭为线性主部加受控非线性余项；截断展开的主项 $\\Theta main$ 在左右边界层取得指定大符号，Lemma 7\.7/7\.8 排除振荡并保证轨道穿越 $\\Delta \_\{1\}$ 或 $\\Delta \_\{2\}$。
4. **主张：** Proof of Theorem 1\.1 通过改变连续参数 $\\alpha \\gamma $ 使 P1–P3 轨道与唯一 $C\\infty $ P3 轨道相交，从而得到通向 P4 的 holosphere 序列。
   - **路线：** 在 $\\alpha \\gamma $ 的两个边界层取样，比较函数 $F\(\\alpha \\gamma \)$ 的符号相反；ODE 对参数连续依赖和介值定理给出 $F=0$ 的 $\\alpha \\gamma \*$，轨道唯一性使其与 P1–P3 轨道重合，另一侧出口再由根分支顺序延伸到 P4，并令 b→0 得 rn→r\+。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦流体方程，主要进展属于存在性；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**AI 声明核查**

已核查未见 AI 披露。

检查范围：PDF 全文第1–50页；缓存全文 2608\.30443\.txt。

**原始英文摘要**

We consider the barotropic Euler equations in dimension $d &gt; 1$ with decaying density at spatial infinity\. The phase portrait of the nonlinear ODE governing the equation for spherically symmetric self-similar solutions has been introduced in the pioneering work of Guderley\. The existence of smooth decaying solutions through the sonic line has been obtained in the work \(F\. Merle et al\., 2022\) in a suitable range of parameters\. We work in this paper in a different range of parameters and show the existence of holosphere like solutions which are smooth through the sonic line, but display vanishing density at a sphere\.

#### Exponential mixing for the stochastic Navier--Stokes equation with localized noise

- **作者：** Ziyu Liu、Shengquan Xiang、Zhifei Zhang
- **arXiv：** [2608\.28964](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28964) · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.28964)
- **分类：** math\.PR、math\.AP、math\.OC
- **进展类型：** 局部噪声驱动 Navier–Stokes 指数混合
- **阅读优先级：** 55/100 · 中优先级
- **分析深度：** 已补读正文关键部分

**完成的工作**

论文研究带 Navier-slip 边界的二维 Navier–Stokes 方程，其中随机力由局部子域上的随机热方程产生。作者把系统提升为 Navier–Stokes–heat Markov 过程，先构造局部线性化方程的低频渐近零控制和弱可观测性，再经正则化控制得到渐近强 Feller，结合 Lyapunov 估计和不可约性证明唯一不变测度与指数混合。

**使用技术**

- Markov 提升与 Lyapunov 估计
- 线性化 Navier–Stokes 局部控制
- 低频截断和弱可观测性
- Malliavin 控制与渐近强 Feller

**可能的突破**

论文将仅在局部热分量注入、且通过时间相关力间接作用于速度的退化噪声，转化为可量化的全耦合稳定化：有限强迫模态经低频控制和热方程提升吸收高频余项，最终足以建立二维系统的唯一不变测度和指数混合。

**限制与不确定性**

结论限于二维有界光滑单连通域、Navier-slip 边界、局部热噪声和前 N 个模态非零；控制与混合依赖 N 随噪声能量上界变化，未覆盖三维或直接局部白噪声。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** Theorem 3\.1 将局部线性化 Navier–Stokes–heat 系统的初态驱动到近似零态，并给出控制范数和终端误差的定量界。
   - **路线：** 先用带额外正则性的 Carleman 控制把线性化速度 z 在时间 1 送至零，再截断到前 N 个热模态并修正端点以构造可由热方程实现的路径；Fenchel–Rockafellar 正则化控制反演 Gramian，得到式\(3\.3\)–\(3\.4\)的终端和控制界。
2. **主张：** Proposition 3\.2、Proposition 3\.6 和 Proposition 3\.7 把局部控制转成仅观测热分量前 N 模态的弱可观测性。
   - **路线：** 对伴随耦合系统使用控制–观测对偶：线性化 Navier–Stokes 控制经低频投影产生趋零终端误差，再通过热路径提升传给耦合状态；选择 $\\delta ∼\\lambda \_N^\{-2\}$ 平衡端点修正与高频耗散，得到初态由有限模态观测加 vanishing remainder 控制的估计。
3. **主张：** Proposition 4\.3 和 Proposition 4\.6 分别建立耦合 Markov 过程的渐近强 Feller 性与不可约性。
   - **路线：** 把 Theorem 3\.1 的正则化控制嵌入 Malliavin 导数，得到梯度估计中随时间衰减的残项，从而验证 asymptotic strong Feller；另一方面利用无强迫系统的耗散稳定性和热噪声高斯支撑，把任意有界初态以正概率送入共同小球，得到弱不可约性。
4. **主张：** Theorem B 和 Theorem A 得到耦合系统唯一不变测度、指数混合以及原始 Navier–Stokes 速度律的指数收敛。
   - **路线：** 以等价能量范数建立指数 Lyapunov 估计，将 Proposition 4\.3/4\.6 与 Hairer–Mattingly 混合判据的三条件合并，得到耦合 Markov 过程的唯一不变测度和式\(1\.7\)；再从热分量的不变高斯律启动并取速度边缘，平均掉 Markov 提升后得到 Theorem A。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦流体方程，主要进展属于控制理论；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**AI 声明核查**

已核查未见 AI 披露。

检查范围：PDF 全文第1–44页（含 Acknowledgement、Appendices 与 References）；缓存全文 2608\.28964\.txt。

**原始英文摘要**

This paper studies the 2D Navier--Stokes equation on a bounded domain with Navier-slip boundary conditions, driven by spatially localized stationary forcing generated by a stochastic heat equation\. We prove exponential mixing for the associated Navier--Stokes--heat system, and consequently exponential convergence of the velocity law under stationary forcing\. The main difficulty is that the white noise is confined to a subdomain and reaches the velocity indirectly through the heat component\. To address the degeneracy, the proof combines Malliavin calculus with PDE control theory\. The key ingredient is a stabilization scheme that converts localized Navier--Stokes controls into time-regular controls compatible with the heat dynamics\.

**原始英文摘要**

We establish local-in-time dispersive and sharp Strichartz estimates for the linear wave equation with Neumann boundary conditions inside a cylindrical convex domain $Ω\\subset \\mathbb\{R\}^3$ featuring a non-empty smooth boundary $\\partialΩ$\. The cylindrical geometry dictates that the nonnegative radius of curvature vanishes along the axial direction\. By utilizing an explicit spectral analysis based on the zeros of the Airy function derivative, alongside a tailored Airy--Poisson summation formula, we capture the microlocal behavior of multi-reflected waves and caustics\. Finally, we apply these sharp Strichartz estimates without loss of derivatives to prove local well-posedness and small-data global well-posedness for the energy-critical quintic nonlinear wave equation \(NLW\) subject to Neumann boundary conditions\.

**原始英文摘要**

Fushchych and Shtelen defined approximate symmetry by expanding the solution of a perturbed differential equation in the small parameter, replacing the original equation by a triangular system for the expansion coefficients\. We extend this construction to arbitrary order\. A coefficient-map formulation yields the order-$p$ Fushchych--Shtelen \(FS\) system and establishes, under explicit hypotheses, a correspondence with the Baikov--Gazizov--Ibragimov \(BGI\) method\. The two frameworks are compared for a perturbed cubic wave equation\. Up to equivalences, we classify the nonlinearities admitting an FS dilation through second order\. The classification consists of a generic power family and logarithmic branches at exceptional exponents\. Among perturbations nontrivial at first order, vertical BGI continuations select a proper subfamily of one FS branch, and a second common branch arises when the perturbation first enters at second order; the two methods are related but not interchangeable\. Joint reduction by the Lorentz algebra and the inherited FS dilation integrates all resulting FS normal forms; the explicit solutions of the 1989 Fushchych--Shtelen letter are recovered as the first-order members of this reduction\. In the part common to both frameworks, the logarithmic corrections arise from the expansion of a power law with a perturbation-dependent exponent\. Finally, periodic travelling waves are used to examine the long-scale validity of the FS expansion\. The first correction is obtained by quadrature, the second-order secular structure is isolated, and phase renormalization produces bounded order-consistent approximations\. For integer-power members of the generic family, the amplitude dependence of the corrected wavenumber agrees with the scaling weights obtained from the symmetry classification\.

**原始英文摘要**

We study the time-dependent Hartree--Fock equation in $\\R^3$ with physical Coulomb interactions in both direct and exchange channels, with exchange coupling $η$, linearized about homogeneous equilibria $γ\_f=g\(-i\\nabla\)$ having compact momentum support\. Nguyen and You showed that, without exchange, two purely imaginary plasmon poles $\\pm iτ\_0\(\|k\|\)$ exist below a survival threshold $κ\_0&gt;0$\. Existing nonlinear Hartree--Fock theory requires a short-range direct interaction and a smooth small exchange kernel, excluding the present Coulomb setting\. On every compact band $0&lt;k\_-\\le\|k\|\\le k\_\+&lt;κ\_0$, we prove unconditionally for Coulomb exchange that, for small $\|η\|$, the poles persist, remain simple and purely imaginary, and depend real-analytically on $η$\. Their first-order shift $τ\_X$ splits into explicit static self-energy and dynamic vertex corrections\. Each pole yields an exact undamped mode with a closed-form unit-density eigenfunction, embedded in the fiber generator's essential spectrum\. The causal density Green function splits into an explicit undamped sine wave and a remainder whose Laplace transform is holomorphic near the poles, while the plasmon channel satisfies uniform $t^\{-3/2\}$ dispersive decay and endpoint Strichartz estimates\. A first-order Ward-type cancellation gives $\|τ\_X\(r\)\|\\le Cr^2$ down to $r=0$; hence exchange leaves the plasma gap unchanged to first order, and we obtain a closed formula for the stiffness correction\. Numerics for a $C^9$ smoothed Fermi ball support the analysis: self-energy and vertex terms cancel within $1\.2\\%$ on the computed range, and exchange softens the dispersion most strongly near $0\.7\\,κ\_0$\.

**原始英文摘要**

We establish sharp endpoint mapping properties for the wave operators $W\_\\pm\(H,-Δ\)$ of two-dimensional Schrödinger operators $H=-Δ\+V$ with real-valued decaying potentials $V$\. Together with the known non-endpoint $L^p$ theory, our results give a complete classification of the $L^p$ mapping properties of the two-dimensional wave operators, and reveal an unexpected reversal of the usual threshold paradigm at the endpoints $p=1$ and $p=\\infty$\. When zero is a regular point of $H$, the wave operators fail to be bounded on $L^1\(\\mathbb\{R\}^2\)$ and on $L^\\infty\(\\mathbb\{R\}^2\)$, but they satisfy the atural substitute estimates of Calderón--Zygmund type: $$ L^1\(\\mathbb\{R\}^2\)\\longrightarrow L^\{1,\\infty\}\(\\mathbb\{R\}^2\),\\ \\ \\ \\mathcal\{H\}^1\(\\mathbb\{R\}^2\)\\longrightarrow L^1\(\\mathbb\{R\}^2\),\\ \\ \\ L^\\infty\(\\mathbb\{R\}^2\)\\longrightarrow \\mathrm\{BMO\}\(\\mathbb\{R\}^2\)\. $$ When zero is instead a threshold singularity of the first kind---an s-wave resonance with no other threshold obstruction, the wave operators are bounded on both endpoint spaces $L^1\(\\mathbb\{R\}^2\)$ and $L^\\infty\(\\mathbb\{R\}^2\)$\. Thus, in dimension two, an s-wave resonance improves the endpoint behavior of the wave operators, in sharp contrast with dimensions $n\\ge3$, where the only regular case is the favorable one\. We also determine the endpoint behavior in the remaining zero-energy spectral configurations of $H$\. A p-wave resonance obstructs both the $L^1$- and the $L^\\infty$-boundedness of the wave operators, while in the zero-eigenvalue case we obtain necessary and sufficient conditions for endpoint boundedness, expressed in terms of the presence of s- and p-wave resonances and of explicit second-order harmonic moment cancellations satisfied by the zero-energy eigenfunctions\.

**原始英文摘要**

We study high-frequency propagation for a viscoelastic wave equation with spatially dependent hereditary memory in relative-history form\. The kernel may have the integrable singularity $\\mathfrak m\(s,x\)=s^\{p-1\}m\(s,x\)$, $0&lt;p&lt;1$; the regular case $p=1$ is included\. Using the half-amplitude propagation distance and corresponding travel time as units, the wavelength $h\\ll1$ yields the memory factor $\\varepsilon=h^\{1-p\}$\. We construct exact solutions with full two-scale geometric-optics expansions in powers $h^\{k\+\(1-p\)\\ell\}$\. Memory modifies the propagation geometry through the instantaneous modulus $σ\+\\varepsilon\\int\_0^\\infty\\mathfrak m\(s,\\cdot\)\\,d s$, while the kernel singularity contributes $C\_p=Γ\(p\)e^\{iπp/2\}$ to the leading transport equation\. For $0&lt;p&lt;1$, this produces fractional scales, frequency-dependent attenuation, and a dispersive phase correction; for $p=1$, the fractional hierarchy disappears, attenuation is frequency independent, and the transport phase correction vanishes\. We also derive a local damped wave equation whose incoming high-frequency solutions approximate the hereditary solutions with $O\(h\)$ error in semiclassical $C^k$ norms\. Exterior observations for all incident directions and $0&lt;h\\ll1$ uniquely recover $σ\_\{\\mathfrak m\}$ and the full temporal jet of $m$ at $s=0$, which determine the expansion modulo $O\(h^\\infty\)$\. Finally, a contraction-semigroup argument gives well-posedness and arbitrary finite-order Sobolev regularity for spatially dependent weakly singular kernels and prescribed full prehistory, with explicit compatibility conditions and estimates uniform in $\\varepsilon$\. These estimates justify the geometric-optics construction\.

We present an alternative proof of the global well-posedness of nonlinear wave equations in three spatial dimensions under the null condition, in the regularity regime of the classical local existence theory, assuming smallness of the angular derivatives\. The purpose of this work is to provide a broader framework for analyzing the inhomogeneous nonlinear Schrödinger equation \(INLS\) \\\[iu\_t \+ u\_\{xx\} \\pm \|x\|^\{-b\}\|u\|^\{α-1\}u=0, \\quad 1&lt;α&lt; 5-2b\\; \\text\{and\}\\; 0&lt; b\\leq 1/4\.\\\] Specifically, we establish low-regularity well-posedness in the Fourier-Lebesgue $\\widehat\{L\}^\{p\}$ spaces for $4/3&lt;p&lt;8$\. The analysis is carried out differently for the cases $p&lt;2$ and $p&gt;2$\. Primarily, in both cases, we prove global well-posedness for arbitrarily large initial data via the data decomposition method adapted for the Fourier-Lebesgue spaces\. Furthermore, we obtain analogous results for INLS in modulation spaces $M^\{p,p'\}$ for $4/3&lt;p&lt;2$\.

**原始英文摘要**

Let \\\(u&gt;0\\\) be the first Dirichlet \\\(p\\\)-eigenfunction on a bounded convex domain \\\(Ω\\subset\\mathbb R^N\\\), and set \\\[ X\_Ω:=\|\\nabla\\log u\|^\{p-2\}\\nabla\\log u\. \\\] We study whether the sharp one-dimensional two-point modulus for \\\(X\_Ω\\\), which for \\\(p=2\\\) reduces to the logarithmic-gradient estimate of Andrews and Clutterbuck, persists for \\\(p\\neq2\\\)\. We prove sharp estimates on intervals and balls for every \\\(p&gt;1\\\), with the radial modulus on balls strictly larger than the one-dimensional one\. In dimensions \\\(N\\ge2\\\), however, the one-dimensional modulus fails on general convex domains for every \\\(p\\neq2\\\)\. For \\\(1&lt;p&lt;2\\\), at every sufficiently small fixed scale there are smooth uniformly convex domains for which the corresponding two-point flux tends to zero\. For \\\(p&gt;2\\\), on thin domains \\\(Ω\_\\varepsilon=D\\times\(-\\varepsilon,\\varepsilon\)\\\), with \\\(D\\subset\\mathbb R^\{N-1\}\\\) bounded and convex, the normalized first eigenfunctions satisfy \\\[ u\_\\varepsilon\(x,\\varepsilon z\)\\longrightarrow \\frac\{φ\(z\)\}\{φ\(0\)\} \\left\(\\frac\{G\_D\(x\)\}\{G\_D\(x\_0\)\}\\right\)^\{2/p\} \\\] locally uniformly in \\\(D\\times\(-1,1\)\\\), where \\\(φ\\\) and \\\(G\_D\\\) are the first Dirichlet eigenfunctions of the \\\(p\\\)-Laplacian on \\\(\(-1,1\)\\\) and of the Laplacian on \\\(D\\\), respectively\. This yields the failure for \\\(p&gt;2\\\)\. Finally, for arbitrary \\\(C^2\\\) functions, positivity of the symmetric differential of the \\\(p\\\)-gradient implies convexity, and the converse holds universally if and only if \\\(p=2\\\)\.

检查范围：缓存全文 2608\.29813\.txt；PDF 末尾区域与全文关键词检索。

**原始英文摘要**

We establish boundary Harnack inequalities for non-negative solutions of the Kolmogorov equation \\\[ \\mathcal Ku=Δ\_xu\+x\\cdot\\nabla\_yu-\\partial\_tu=0 \\\] in non-characteristic intrinsic Lipschitz graph domains whose defining functions may depend on the higher-order variable \\\(y\_m\\\)\. Earlier theory required independence of \\\(y\_m\\\)\. We replace this symmetry by quantitative asymptotic cylindricality, measured by a scale-invariant \\\(C^\{0,1/3\}\_\{y\_m\}\\\) defect\. The perturbative comparison theorem applies whenever this defect is sufficiently small, and hence at all sufficiently small scales for \\\(C^\{0,α\}\_\{y\_m\}\\\) graphs with \\\(α&gt;1/3\\\), as well as under a vanishing critical cylindrical modulus\. We obtain local comparability and intrinsic Hölder continuity of quotients of positive solutions, together with a Hölder estimate for their logarithmic quotient\. The proof combines local boundary estimates, valid without \\\(y\_m\\\)-independence, with a global rigidity argument for cylindrical blow-up limits\. The central issue is uniform balance of forward and backward reference values\. If this balance fails at collapsing scales, maximal-scale selection and a second rescaling produce a non-negative polynomial-growth Dirichlet solution in an unbounded cylindrical domain that vanishes at the backward reference point but not at the forward one\. We exclude this configuration using boundary-energy and mean-value estimates, finite-dimensionality of spaces of polynomial-growth solutions, and translation invariance in \\\(y\_m\\\)\. The resulting finite-dimensional translation representation yields real-analytic dependence on \\\(y\_m\\\)\. Propagation of zeros, analytic continuation along invariant fibres, and a Tikhonov uniqueness argument then force the limiting solution to vanish identically\. The exponent \\\(1/3\\\) is critical because \\\(y\_m\\\) has homogeneous degree three\.

**原始英文摘要**

Let $u$ be the torsion function for the Ornstein-Uhlenbeck operator on a bounded domain $Ω\\subset \\mathbb\{R\}^n$, i\.e\., the solution of $Δu - x \\cdot \\nabla u = -1$ in $Ω$ with $u = 0$ on $\\partialΩ$\. Let $T\_γ\(Ω\) = \\int\_Ωu \\, dγ$ be the Gaussian torsional rigidity\. We prove that the Brunn-Minkowski-type inequality $T\_γ\(\(1-t\)Ω\_0 \+ tΩ\_1\)^α \\le \(1-t\) T\_γ\(Ω\_0\)^α \+ t T\_γ\(Ω\_1\)^α$ fails for every exponent $α&gt; 0$ and in every dimension $n \\ge 2$, for a pair of convex bodies centrally symmetric with respect to the origin, which may be taken smooth with positive curvature\. This answers Conjecture 1\.4 for any $n \\geq 2$, and Question~\(Q\), of Marín Sola and Salerno in the negative\. The mechanism is a first-order lower bound for $T\_γ$ at a ball $Ω\_0$ under Minkowski perturbations\. When the perturbing body $Ω\_1$ has small torsion and large mean width, $T\_γ\(\(1-t\)Ω\_0 \+ tΩ\_1\)$ increases to first order\. Since the Minkowski combination has larger torsion than both endpoints, no exponent can repair the inequality\. For $n=1$, convexity with the optimal exponent $1/3$ holds on symmetric intervals by results of the same authors, \\cite\{MSS26\}\. We prove that the logarithm of the torsion is neither convex nor concave along Minkowski combinations of symmetric intervals, that no non-zero exponent yields concavity, and that convexity fails for every positive exponent when one set is a union of two intervals or when the sets are reflected off-center intervals\.

**原始英文摘要**

Starting from localized energy estimates, we prove finite speed of propagation for kinetic solutions to stochastic porous media equations with nonlinear conservative noise, the existence and uniqueness of which has recently been established\. In particular, we propose a novel iteration technique which allows us to obtain a Stampacchia-type inequality involving one single integral quantity, despite the possibly different scaling behaviors of the porous media and the noise term\. This allows us to apply stochastic filtering arguments developed for the case of linear source-type noise\. Using related ideas, we identify flatness conditions on initial data which guarantee locally the occurrence of a waiting time phenomenon, i\.e\., the onset of forward propagation of the solution's support is locally delayed\. The condition for the latter matches the one for the deterministic porous media equation up to a logarithmic correction in the case of critical nonlinearity in the noise, but it requires more and more flatness of the initial data as the nonlinearity tends towards linear conservative noise\. This is in line with the expected behavior: In the case of linear conservative noise, instantaneous forward motion is possible due to the effects of stochastic transport, no matter how flat the initial profile is\.

论文研究 Robin 大尺度极限的唯一性、反应族一致性和参数均匀曲率修正，核心是边界层半空间谱问题。

**使用技术**

- 弱最大值原理
- 半空间约化
- Robin–Lieb localization
- 边界层谱隙
- 渐近展开

**可能的突破**

Theorems 1\.1–1\.3 分别给出 uniform uniqueness、反应族一致性和曲率修正。

**限制与不确定性**

依赖 Robin 参数、反应项条件和边界局部曲率；大尺度极限按文中 compact reaction family 处理。

**证明逻辑/大纲**

1. **主张：** Lemma 2\.1 提供 Robin 算子的 bounded weak maximum principle。
   - **路线：** 对截断正部测试弱方程，利用椭圆性和 Robin 边界符号控制负部，从而建立唯一性所需的比较原理。
2. **主张：** Lemma 2\.2 与 Theorem 2\.3 将边界层约化到半空间谱问题。
   - **路线：** 通过法向缩放把曲率边界局部化为半空间，利用 Fourier/变分比较得到 potential-bearing Robin–Lieb localization。
3. **主张：** Proposition 3\.1 与 Theorems 1\.1–1\.3 完成大尺度渐近。
   - **路线：** 显式计算半空间 profile 的平移公式和谱隙，将局部误差与曲率项积分回原域，得到均匀唯一性和参数修正。

**排序理由**

论文聚焦其他分析方向，主要进展属于分析结果；排序综合考虑其与 PDE 机制、可复用估计、正则性和长时间行为的相关程度。

**AI 声明核查**

已核查未见 AI 披露。

检查范围：缓存全文 2608\.30211\.txt；PDF 末尾区域与全文关键词检索。

**原始英文摘要**

Berestycki and Graham proved large-dilation uniqueness for bounded positive solutions of $-Δu=f\(u\)$ in $κΩ$, with $u\+α\\partial\_νu=0$ on $\\partial\(κΩ\)$, when $α$ is fixed, and remarked that the dilation threshold should not depend on $α$\. We show that it does not, including at the Dirichlet and Neumann endpoints, for possibly unbounded uniformly $C^\{2,γ\}$ domains\. The half-space linearizations retain a common positive spectral gap across the compactified boundary parameter\. The remaining endpoint difficulty is nonlinear compactness at the Dirichlet end, where the Robin coefficient diverges\. Rescaling at its reciprocal scale makes the limiting equation harmonic\. Any trace that persisted in this limit would give a bounded harmonic half-space solution of $\\partial\_νv\+v=0$, which a Liouville lemma rules out\. Combining this endpoint compactness with the half-space gap and localization gives uniform large-dilation uniqueness\. When $\\partialΩ\\neq\\varnothing$, we further obtain convergence of the spectral bottom to its half-space value with error $O\(κ^\{-1/2\}\)$\. For bounded $C^\{4,γ\}$ domains the boundary layer also has a first mean-curvature correction, with remainder $O\(κ^\{-1-γ\}\+κ^\{-2\}\)$ on each fixed boundary strip\.
